106:Tb3b,<emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p><var>callback</var> should be a function that accepts two arguments. <code>groupBy</code> calls <var>callback</var> once for each element in <var>items</var>, in ascending order, and constructs a new object. Each value returned by <var>callback</var> is coerced to a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref>. For each such <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref>, the result object has a property whose key is that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> and whose value is an array containing all the elements for which the <var>callback</var> return value coerced to that key.</p>
          <p><var>callback</var> is called with two arguments: the value of the element and the index of the element.</p>
          <p>The return value of <code>groupBy</code> is an object that does not inherit from <emu-intrinsic>%Object.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
        <p>This function performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>groups</var> be ? <emu-xref aoid="GroupBy"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-groupby">GroupBy</a></emu-xref>(<var>items</var>, <var>callback</var>, <emu-const>property</emu-const>).</li><li>Let <var>obj</var> be <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryObjectCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate">OrdinaryObjectCreate</a></emu-xref>(<emu-val>null</emu-val>).</li><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> { <var class="field">[[Key]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Elements]]</var> } <var>g</var> of <var>groups</var>, do<ol><li>Let <var>elements</var> be <emu-xref aoid="CreateArrayFromList"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-createarrayfromlist">CreateArrayFromList</a></emu-xref>(<var>g</var>.<var class="field">[[Elements]]</var>).</li><li>Perform ! <emu-xref aoid="CreateDataPropertyOrThrow"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-createdatapropertyorthrow">CreateDataPropertyOrThrow</a></emu-xref>(<var>obj</var>, <var>g</var>.<var class="field">[[Key]]</var>, <var>elements</var>).</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>obj</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

113:T6ae,<p>The <dfn>Function prototype object</dfn>:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>is <dfn><emu-intrinsic>%Function.prototype%</emu-intrinsic></dfn>.</li>
        <li>is itself a built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>.</li>
        <li>accepts any arguments and returns <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> when invoked.</li>
        <li>does not have a <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method; it cannot be used as a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref> with the <code>new</code> operator.</li>
        <li>has a <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> internal slot whose value is <emu-intrinsic>%Object.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>.</li>
        <li>does not have a <emu-val>"prototype"</emu-val> property.</li>
        <li>has a <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property whose value is <emu-val>+0</emu-val><sub>𝔽</sub>.</li>
        <li>has a <emu-val>"name"</emu-val> property whose value is the empty String.</li>
      </ul>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The Function prototype object is specified to be a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> to ensure compatibility with ECMAScript code that was created prior to the ECMAScript 2015 specification.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      114:Tccb,<p>This method performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>func</var> be the <emu-val>this</emu-val> value.</li><li class="exit">If <emu-xref aoid="IsCallable"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-iscallable">IsCallable</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>) is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</li><li>If <var>argArray</var> is either <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="PrepareForTailCall"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-preparefortailcall">PrepareForTailCall</a></emu-xref>().</li><li class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="Call"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-call">Call</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>, <var>thisArg</var>).</li></ol></li><li>Let <var>argList</var> be ? <emu-xref aoid="CreateListFromArrayLike"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-createlistfromarraylike">CreateListFromArrayLike</a></emu-xref>(<var>argArray</var>).</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="PrepareForTailCall"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-preparefortailcall">PrepareForTailCall</a></emu-xref>().</li><li id="step-function-proto-apply-call" class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="Call"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-call">Call</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>, <var>thisArg</var>, <var>argList</var>).</li></ol></emu-alg>
        <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>The <var>thisArg</var> value is passed without modification as the <emu-val>this</emu-val> value. This is a change from Edition 3, where an <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> or <emu-val>null</emu-val> <var>thisArg</var> is replaced with the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref aoid="ToObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-toobject">ToObject</a></emu-xref> is applied to all other values and that result is passed as the <emu-val>this</emu-val> value. Even though the <var>thisArg</var> is passed without modification, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function">non-strict functions</a></emu-xref> still perform these transformations upon entry to the function.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
        <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>If <var>func</var> is either an arrow function or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object">bound function exotic object</a></emu-xref>, then the <var>thisArg</var> will be ignored by the function <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> in step <emu-xref href="#step-function-proto-apply-call"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#step-function-proto-apply-call">6</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

11a:T4ce,<p>Every Function instance is an ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> and has the internal slots listed in <emu-xref href="#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects">Table 25</a></emu-xref>. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">Function objects</a></emu-xref> created using the <code>Function.prototype.bind</code> method (<emu-xref href="#sec-function.prototype.bind"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-function.prototype.bind">20.2.3.2</a></emu-xref>) have the internal slots listed in <emu-xref href="#table-internal-slots-of-bound-function-exotic-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-internal-slots-of-bound-function-exotic-objects">Table 26</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <p>Function instances have the following properties:</p>

      11b:T5d1,<p>The value of the <emu-val>"name"</emu-val> property <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type">is a String</a></emu-xref> that is descriptive of the function. The name has no semantic significance but is typically a variable or <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name">property name</a></emu-xref> that is used to refer to the function at its point of definition in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text">ECMAScript source text</a></emu-xref>. This property has the attributes { <var class="field">[[Writable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Enumerable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Configurable]]</var>: <emu-val>true</emu-val> }.</p>
        <p>Anonymous <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> that do not have a contextual name associated with them by this specification use the empty String as the value of the <emu-val>"name"</emu-val> property.</p>

11d:T44a,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> abstract operation HostHasSourceTextAvailable takes argument <var>func</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean. It allows <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref> to prevent the source text from being provided for <var>func</var>.</p>
      <p>An implementation of HostHasSourceTextAvailable must conform to the following requirements:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>It must be deterministic with respect to its parameters. Each time it is called with a specific <var>func</var> as its argument, it must return the same result.</li>
      </ul>
      <p>The default implementation of HostHasSourceTextAvailable is to return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</p>

        <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">Function objects</a></emu-xref> created using <code>Function.prototype.bind</code> are <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic objects</a></emu-xref>.         <p>This property has the attributes { <var class="field">[[Writable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Enumerable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Configurable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val> }.</p>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>This is the default implementation of <code>%Symbol.hasInstance%</code> that most functions inherit. <code>%Symbol.hasInstance%</code> is called by the <code>instanceof</code> operator to determine whether a value is an instance of a specific <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref>. An expression such as</p>
          <pre><code class="hljs javascript">
            v <span class="hljs-keyword">instanceof</span> F
          </code></pre>
          <p>evaluates as</p>
          <pre><code class="hljs javascript">
            F[%<span class="hljs-title class_">Symbol</span>.<span class="hljs-property">hasInstance</span>%](v)
          </code></pre>
          <p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref> function can control which objects are recognized as its instances by <code>instanceof</code> by exposing a different <code>%Symbol.hasInstance%</code> method on the function.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
        <p>This property is non-writable and non-configurable to prevent tampering that could be used to globally expose the target function of a bound function.</p>
        <p>The value of the <emu-val>"name"</emu-val> property of this method is <emu-val>"[Symbol.hasInstance]"</emu-val>.</p>

143:T47b,<p>AggregateError instances are <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref> that inherit properties from their <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-properties-of-the-aggregate-error-prototype-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-properties-of-the-aggregate-error-prototype-objects">AggregateError prototype object</a></emu-xref> and have an <var class="field">[[ErrorData]]</var> internal slot whose value is <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>. The only specified use of <var class="field">[[ErrorData]]</var> is by <code>Object.prototype.toString</code> (<emu-xref href="#sec-object.prototype.tostring"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-object.prototype.tostring">20.1.3.6</a></emu-xref>) and <code>Error.isError</code> (<emu-xref href="#sec-error.iserror"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-error.iserror">20.5.2.1</a></emu-xref>) to identify Error, AggregateError, or <var>NativeError</var> instances.</p>
